How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

(part 40/96)

# Salvaging
Aliases: Salvage, Salvaged
Categories: Mechanics
[Salvaging]
Salvaging is a mechanic that lets players trade certain items for [[Essence]] and other items.
## Salvaging Mechanics
Items can be Salvaged at certain NPCs: [[Malik]], [[Marigold]], [[Gemma]], [[Alwin]], [[Igor]], [[Spider Tamer]], [[Frozen Alex]], [[Tyzzo]], [[Researcher Beryl]] and [[Rosemary]].
[[Star Upgrades|Starred]] [[Dungeon Items]] grant +2 [[Essence]] of the same type for each star. [[Dungeon Items]] with +50% [[Dungeon Items|Stat Boost]] grant +10 [[Essence]] of the same type.
Items upgraded with [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] will refund 1/2 of spent resources back, rounded down.
[[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] items that were donated to the [[Museum]] cannot be salvaged.
## Salvagable Items
### Essence
Certain items can be salvaged to grant [[Essence]]. This amount can be increased by up to 20% with the [[Frozen Alex|Cooled Forges]] perk, purchasable at [[Frozen Alex]].

There is also a 45% chance for bonus items to be rolled, which can be increased by up to 10% with the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Endermite Pet]]'s Sacrificer ability. Amount of [[Essence]] granted is not affected by [[Frozen Alex|Cooled Forges]] perk.

# Skill XP Calculation
Categories: Mechanics, Skills, Pages with math equations
The [[Skills|Skill XP]] of a player depends on various stats and factors. Following is a formula for each [[Skills|Skill]] type used to calculate the XP given.
## Formula
  BaseExp×1+Wisdom100×MultiplicativeBoosts
Where:
- BaseExp is the base amount of Exp given.
- Wisdom is amount of [[Wisdom]] for the skill.
- MultiplicativeBoosts is the boosts rarely obtained from certain sources, like: [[Derpy]] or [[Skill Experience Bonus]].

# SkyBlock API
Aliases: API
Categories: Mechanics
[SkyBlock API]
The Hypixel SkyBlock API is an external resource used to access a vast array of SkyBlock-related data beyond the confines of the game itself. As an integral component of the official Hypixel Public API, it provides automated access to various essential features, including detailed information on auctions, Bazaar data, and comprehensive player profiles encompassing statistics, skills, collections, and inventories.
## Accessing the API
Accessing the Hypixel SkyBlock API is a straightforward process that grants users the ability to retrieve a wealth of SkyBlock-related information. To get started, the official API documentation can be accessed at https://api.hypixel.net/. This comprehensive resource provides detailed documentation on the available endpoints, parameters, and response formats, offering a solid foundation for utilizing the API effectively.
In order to access certain API endpoints and maximize the benefits of the API, acquiring an API key is recommended. An API key can be obtained by going to https://developer.hypixel.net/dashboard/ and clicking "Create API Key". By doing so, users can generate an API key that comes with a default rate limit of 300 requests per 5 minutes, but will expire in 72 hours (3 days). To obtain a permanent key, you must click "Create App" and select "Personal API Key", which will grant you a key that provides the same default rate limit, but will not expire on its own. This key serves as the gateway to unrestricted access to the API's vast array of features and data.
However, it's important to note that not all API endpoints require an API key. Several endpoints, such as those providing static or semi-static data like active auctions or Bazaar data, can be accessed without the need for an API key. This ensures that even users without an API key can still access valuable information and utilize it for their own purposes.
### Converting Minecraft Username to UUID
Any parameter that represents a player or a SkyBlock profile should be filled with the UUID, not the player name. The official Mojang API can be used to convert a player name to the corresponding player UUID:
https://api.mojang.com/users/profiles/minecraft/Player Name
### Parsing Inventory Data
Inventory data in the SkyBlock API is provided in the form of GZip compressed NBT (Named Binary Tag) data, which is then base64 encoded. To work with this inventory data, there are numerous libraries available for most programming languages that simplify the process of handling NBT data. These libraries provide convenient methods and functions specifically designed for parsing, decoding, and manipulating NBT data structures.
## SkyBlock Resources Endpoints
The SkyBlock Resources Endpoints in the Hypixel Networth SkyBlock API can be accessed without requiring an API key. This means that users can retrieve valuable information about active auctions, Bazaar rates, and other static resources without the need for authentication using an API key.
### Collections
Information regarding Collections in SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/resources/skyblock/collections
### Skills
Information regarding skills in SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/resources/skyblock/skills
### Items
Information regarding items in SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/resources/skyblock/items
### Elections and Mayors
Information regarding the current mayor and ongoing election in SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/resources/skyblock/election
### Bingo
Information regarding the current bingo event and its goals:
https://api.hypixel.net/resources/skyblock/bingo
### Auctions
Returns the currently active auctions sorted by last updated first and paginated:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/auctions
SkyBlock auctions which ended in the last 60 seconds:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/auctions_ended
### Bazaar
Returns the list of products along with their sell summary, buy summary and quick status:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/bazaar
### Fire Sales
Retrieve the currently active or upcoming Fire Sales for SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/firesales
## Main SkyBlock Endpoints
The SkyBlock Endpoints in the Hypixel Networth SkyBlock API require authentication through an API key. This means that in order to access these endpoints and retrieve specific SkyBlock-related data such as player profiles, stats, collections, and inventories, users must provide a valid API key for authorization.
### Player Profiles
SkyBlock profile data, such as stats, objectives etc. The data returned can differ depending on the players in-game API settings:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/profiles?key=API_KEY&uuid=UUID
### Profile by Profile ID
SkyBlock profile data, such as stats, objectives etc. The data returned can differ depending on the players in-game API settings:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/profile?key=API_KEY&profile=PROFILE_ID
### Bingo Data
Bingo data for participated events of the provided player:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/bingo?key=API_KEY&uuid=UUID
### Auctions by the auction UUID, player UUID, or profile UUID
Information about auctions.:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/auction?key=API_KEY&uuid=AUCTION_UUID
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/auction?key=API_KEY&player=UUID
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/auction?key=API_KEY&profile=PROFILE_ID
### News
Information regarding latest updates in SkyBlock:
https://api.hypixel.net/skyblock/news?key=API_KEY
### Location
The current online status and location of a specific player:
https://api.hypixel.net/status?key=API KEY&uuid=UUID

# Time Systems
Aliases: AE, Hypixel Server Time, SkyBlock Century, SkyBlock Day, SkyBlock Hour, SkyBlock Month, SkyBlock Season, SkyBlock Time, SkyBlock Year, Summer, Time, Time System, Year
Categories: Mechanics
SkyBlock Time [[Time Systems|🛈]]
Main Server
:
of , Year
[[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]]
:
of , Year
SkyBlock Time is one of the time systems used on [[SkyBlock]]. It is a one-timezone system and is used on Hypixel SkyBlock servers. The time is displayed on the message board on the right when the player is logged onto SkyBlock.
Earth Time, sometimes known as Real Life Time, is the date and time system used on Earth. It consists of multiple timezones. For a specific timezone, for example UTC, the time system can be known as "UTC Time".
Hypixel Server Time is the local Earth Time of Hypixel's game servers, which observe Eastern Time. It is another time system used on Hypixel SkyBlock servers.
## SkyBlock Time
### Time Measurement
Time measurement in SkyBlock is colloquially known as "SkyBlock <duration unit>", for example "SkyBlock Day". It is different from time measurement on Earth.
Similarly, time measurement on Earth is colloquially known as "Earth <duration unit>", for example "Earth Day". The measurements of duration in Earth's time system does not differ in different timezones.
Measurement of SkyBlock Duration:
Similar to Time System on Earth: 1 SkyBlock Minutes is 60 SkyBlock Seconds. 1 SkyBlock Hour is 60 SkyBlock Minutes. 1 SkyBlock Day is 24 SkyBlock Hours.
Different from Time System on Earth: 1 SkyBlock Month consists of exactly 31 SkyBlock Days. 1 SkyBlock Season lasts for exactly 3 SkyBlock Months, and is divided into three equal parts named "Early", "(Mid)", and "Late", each lasting for 1 SkyBlock Month. For example, Spring is divided into Early Spring, Spring, and Late Spring. 1 SkyBlock Year consists of exactly 12 SkyBlock Months of 31 SkyBlock Days, or a total of 372 SkyBlock Days.
Multi-year counting: 1 SkyBlock Decade consists of 10 SkyBlock Years. 1 SkyBlock Century consists of 100 SkyBlock Years. etc.
### Relationship with Earth's Time
Since a SkyBlock Year lasts for 5 Days and 4 Hours, a certain SkyBlock date can cycle through 6 different Earth Times, each 4 hours apart. This leads to events suitable for different timezones at different occurrences.
On Main Server:
- The SkyBlock Epoch, time of the beginning of SkyBlock, is defined to be 1st of Early Spring, Year 1 at 00:00, which converts to Jun 11, 2019 at 17:55:00 (UTC), or unix time of 1560275700.
- All SkyBlock days start at either :55, :15 or :35 in Real Life Time. (:MM represents the minute in Real Life Time)
On Alpha Network:
The time system on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] may vary from that of the Main Server, as detailed in [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Hypixel Network#Time on Alpha Network]].
### Conversion with Earth Time
|SkyBlock Time|Real Life Time Seconds|Real Life Time Minutes|Real Life Time Hours|Real Life Time Days|Real Life Time|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|1 Hour|50|0.8333|0.0138|0.0005|50 Seconds|
|1 Day|1,200|20|0.333|0.013|20 Minutes|
|1 Month|37,200|620|10.333|0.430|10 Hours and 20 Minutes|
|1 Season; (3 Months)|111,600|1,860|31|1.2917|1 Day and 7 Hours|
|1 Year|446,400|7,440|124|5.167|5 Days and 4 Hours|
|1 Century|46,440,000|744,000|12,400|516.667|1 Year and 5 Months|
### Occurrences based on SkyBlock Time
Some occurrences in SkyBlock are based on SkyBlock Time. The following are some examples:
|Event|SkyBlock Time|SkyBlock Duration|
|-|-|-|
|☼ Daytime|6:00 AM – 7:00 PM|13 Hours|
|☾ Nighttime|7:00 PM – 6:00 AM|11 Hours|
[Crimson Isle]
|Ghasts ([[Ghast]]) spawn|9:00 PM – 5:00 AM|8 Hours|
[Spooky Festival]
|[[Spooky]] Mobs ([[Spooky]]) spawn|☾ Nighttime|11 Hours|
|[[Headless Horseman]] can be spawned|☾ Nighttime|11 Hours|
[Others]
|[[Unincorporated]] Skeletons ([[Skeleton]]) spawn|☾ Nighttime|11 Hours|
### AE
AE is a year notation used in SkyBlock's [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], it stands for Ancient Era. There is no confirmation what year it is now in this system, nor what happened in year 0. It is likely currently between the year 1533 and 2111.
#### Dated Events

Kaeman helps Lathrop in fulfilling his potential and he becomes [[The Watcher]]. At [[The Wither King]]'s request, The Watcher created a world for Kaeman to live through his happier times again. The Watcher wrote a lot of journals to be scattered around The Catacombs. "Losing" his master, Necron is having doubts about his conquest to world destruction.|1143|

Everyone is killed very quickly, except Solomon who is absorbed by [[Storm]] as his third head and Sadan who is recruited by The Watcher. Later, presumably the same year, Necron sent [[Storm]] to destroy the castle, to prevent them from getting in the way of their plans for world domination. The castle that survived the Calamity ([[Jythar]]) and [[The Wither King]] was now in [[Ruins]] and the Mage Academy was swallowed by void. Realizing that he has created not just an army, but a community Necron he questions his desire for conquest.|1531|

|Until we meet again ([[Journal]])|The year that the adventurers enter dungeons, defeat the Withers, and The Watcher writes a letter ([[Journal]]) to the adventurers, hoping to see them again.|???|
## Hypixel Server Time
### Time Zone
Hypixel's game servers use the Eastern Time Zone (ET), which is either 4 or 5 hours behind UTC, according to U.S. daylight saving time. Hypixel Inc. is incorporated in Quebec, Canada, which observes Eastern Time. Hypixel's game servers are hosted in Chicago, Illinois, USA, which observes Central Time, but the events listed below still occur according to Eastern Time.
### Occurrences based on Hypixel Server Time
Some occurrences in SkyBlock are based on Hypixel Server Time/Eastern Time, including the following.
- [[Fetchur]]'s quest item resets daily at midnight Eastern Time.
- The calculation of [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Helmet]] Donation Cost uses the day of the year based on Eastern Time.
- When the [[Advent Calendar]] is available, the rewards for each day become available at midnight Eastern Time.
## Trivia
- During [[Thorn]]'s bossfight, the crowd mentions the year 2020.
- On 9 April 2024, the Alpha server had a different time offset from what it previously had, presumably because the content being tested was only available during the first quarter of each SkyBlock year (spring). It was modified to be exactly 210 SkyBlock days (70 hours) ahead of the regular server, and the server was opened to the public around the start of Early Spring 2, Year 343. The time offset was later changed on an unknown date (June 10, 2024 at the latest) to match the main server exactly.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added SkyBlock Time.|

# Trading
Categories: Mechanics
[Trading]
Trading is a feature that allows players to trade items and [[Coins]] with each other.
## Overview
### Initiating a Trade
To initiate a trade with another player, the player can either:
- use the /trade <player> command;
- interact with the person they wish to trade with through ⇧ Shift + Right Click;
- or click on the Emerald in their profile menu, which will initiate a trade request.
The recipient will receive the following message in chat:
<username> has sent you a trade request. Click here to accept!
The maximum distance you can trade from is 9 blocks.
### Tradeable Items
Almost all items can be traded, alongside a given lump-sum of [[Coins]]. Some items that cannot be put up for auction on the [[Auction House]], such as [[Minions]] and certain items from [[Anita]], can be traded; other items cannot be auctioned or traded at all, such as [[Sacks]].
Items that are [[Soulbound]] cannot be traded at all, and items that are [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] cannot be traded with players outside of the player's [[Co-op]].
### Trading Coins
A given lump-sum of [[Coins]] can be traded in the Trading menu. Players under [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 50]] have a daily trading limit of 50,000,000 [[Coins]] players above [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 50]] have a limit of 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]], and players above [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 100]] have a limit of 10,000,000,000 [[Coins]].
Different icons in the Trading menu show different amounts of [[Coins]]
- represents 1–99,999 [[Coins]];
- represents 100,000–9,999,999 [[Coins]];
- represents ≥10,000,000 [[Coins]].
### Restrictions
[[Ironman|♻ Ironman]], [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], and [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] players are explicitly restricted from trading. Additionally, players using a nickname cannot trade with anyone other than players with the 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] rank.
## Trivia
- The first time a player completes a trade, they will get the [[Achievements|Businessman]] achievement.
- Accepting a trade request in the [[Rift Dimension]] results in the players swapping ("trading") places rather than trading items, unless they are in the same [[Co-op]].

# Weather
Aliases: Acid Rain, Ashfall, Blossoming, Hellstorm, Moonfall, Rain, Rockfall, Snowstorm, Tropical Rain, Voidstorm, Wispfall
Categories: Mechanics
[Weather]
Mild Weather
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Extreme Weather
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Weather is a mechanic that grants stat boosts and effects on certain [[Locations|Public Islands]].
## Weather Mechanics
Mild Weather condition will start every 60 minutes and lasts for 20 minutes, between XX:35 and XX:55 in real time (UTC). Every 3rd Mild Weather condition will instead be EXTREME. All Weather conditions grant stat boosts, and EXTREME weather conditions have an additional bonus effect (with [[Spider's Den]] and [[Lotus Atoll]] also having a bonus effect during Mild Weather).
[[Professor Wynd]] can also notify the player of the upcoming weather when added as an [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].

|[[Umberella]]|x2 item effect during any Weather|
## Other Locations
### Birch Park
The player can pay [[Vanessa]] 5,000 [[Coins]] to create rain in the [[Birch Park]] for 60 seconds. Up to 30 minutes of rain can be bought.
While raining, players gain +25 [[Fishing Speed]]. Additionally, every 11 seconds there is a 66.6% (2 in 3 rolls) chance to spawn 1-2 [[Squid|Squids]] in water if there are less than 5 [[Squid|Squids]] alive.
### Private Island
Rain and thunderstorms (or snowfall if a [[Biome Stick|Taiga Biome Stick]] is used) occurs randomly on the [[Private Island]]. Weather can be changed with a [[Weather Wand]], or fully disabled with a [[Weather Node]] or by switching the [[Private Island]] biome with a [[Biome Stick|Nether Biome Stick]], [[Biome Stick|Desert Biome Stick]], [[Biome Stick|Mesa Biome Stick]], or [[Biome Stick|Savanna Biome Stick]].
Weather provides no bonuses on the [[Private Island]].
### Ancient Pig
While the [[Ancient Pig]] was active, [[Thunder (event)|Thunderstorms]] could occur and lasted for 20 minutes. During a thunderstorm, [[Charged Creeper|Charged Creepers]] and [[Skeleton Horse|Skeleton Horses]] could spawn.
## Trivia
- Weather was revamped by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] ALAND_ as part of the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
- Due to extreme weather occurring every 3 hours, it always appears at the same times every day.
## Bugs
- Mist extends to the [[Fossil Research Center]], while Snowstorm does not overlap with it. This renders the ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] boost from Snowstorm useless towards the [[Fossil Excavator]].

# Automated Shipping
Aliases: Minion Hoppers
Categories: Minion Modifiers, Automated shipping items, Mechanics
[Automated Shipping]
Automated Shipping is a feature that lets [[Minions]] automatically sell items for [[Coins]] when they are full.
## Overview
There are 2 items that pertain to Automated Shipping, which can be placed in the Automated Shipping slot:
|Slot|Name|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Budget Hopper]]|[[Budget Hopper]]|Sells items for 50% of their NPC sell price.|
|[[Enchanted Hopper]]|[[Enchanted Hopper]]|Sells items for 70% of their NPC sell price.|
Additionally, a third, unobtainable item exists in the API, but is inaccessible.
|[[Removed Coming Soon features]]|Perfect Hopper ([[Removed Coming Soon features]])|Sells items for 100% of their NPC sell price.|

# Minion Crystal
Aliases: Minion Crystals
Categories: Minion Modifiers
Minion Crystals can be placed on a [[Private Island]] to increase the speed of all [[Minions]] of a certain type.
## Mechanic
- Minion Crystals can be placed anywhere on a [[Private Island]].
- Only one minion crystal of each type can be placed down at any time and they can't be sold. Therefore, it is advised to craft only one of each type.
  - Because [[Farm Crystal]] upgrades to [[Cornucopia Crystal]], they are considered the same type and cannot be placed at the same time.
## List of Minion Crystals

|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Farm Crystal]]|[[Farm Crystal]]|Farming Minions ([[Minions]])|+10% speed|Pumpkin VIII (8) ([[Pumpkin]])|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] x12 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Farm Crystal]]|
|[[Woodcutting Crystal]]|[[Woodcutting Crystal]]|Foraging Minions ([[Minions]])|+10% speed|Spruce Log VII (7) ([[Spruce Log]])|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spruce Log]] x12 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Woodcutting Crystal]]|
|[[Mithril Crystal]]|[[Mithril Crystal]]|Mining Minions ([[Minions]])|+10% speed|Mithril IV (4) ([[Mithril]])|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] → [[Mithril Crystal]]|
|[[Winter Crystal]]|[[Winter Crystal]]|Snow Minions ([[Snow Minion]]),; Ice Minions ([[Ice Minion]])|+5%|Red Gifts ([[Red Gift]])||
|[[Cornucopia Crystal]]|[[Cornucopia Crystal]]|Farming Minions ([[Minions]])|Increases the speed of all [[Farming]] [[Minions]] by 10%. Each unique [[Farming]] Minion ([[Minions]]) grants an additional 1% speed bonus.|[[Crafting]]|Recipe: [[Cornucopia]] x8 ×8 + [[Farm Crystal]] → [[Cornucopia Crystal]]|

# Minion Fuel
Aliases: Fuels, Minion Fuels
Categories: Minion Modifiers, Pages with math equations
[Minion Fuel]
Minion Fuel is used to increase the output of [[Minions]]. Depending on the fuel, it lasts for either a limited amount of time or forever. [[Inferno Minion Fuel|Inferno Minion Fuels]] can also make the minion generate more drops, but they can only be used on [[Inferno Minion]].
## Mechanic
A maximum of 1 type of Minion Fuel can be added to a minion, with a maximum of one stack for limited duration fuels, or a maximum of 1 for infinite duration fuels.
Minion Fuels with an infinite duration can be retrieved from a minion at any time. If a minion has at least two of a limited stackable fuel in its slot, the fuel can be partially refunded: one item in the stack (the one currently being used) will be destroyed, while the remaining fuel will be refunded.
Picking up a placed minion also refunds the fuel with the same system: fuels with an unlimited duration will be retrieved, while fuels with limited duration will have the first item in the stack destroyed, while any remaining limited fuel is refunded.
For example if a minion has 5x [[Catalyst|Catalysts]], you can click on the active fuel (or pick up the minion) to destroy 1 Catalyst, while refunding the remaining 4.
## List of Fuels
Below is a list of Minion Fuels, along with how long they last (real-time), the buff they give to the Minion's speed, how they are obtained, and the price on the [[Bazaar]]. If the item is not sold on the Bazaar, the price shown is the price to craft the item with materials bought from the Bazaar.
Note that the Minion's Time Between Actions is not decreased by the same percent as the speed increase. For more information, see § Calculations below.

## Calculations
Fuels increase the speed of the Minion by X%, which means that the Minion's Time Between Actions is decreased according to the following formula:
Time Between Actions=Base Time Between Actions100%+Speed Increase
### Example Calculation
For a [[Clay Minion|Clay Minion XI]] with [[Enchanted Coal]], the Base Time Between Actions is 16 seconds, and the speed increase from the [[Enchanted Coal]] is +10%, so the new Time Between Actions would be as follows:
Time Between Actions=16seconds100%+10%≈14.55seconds
## Notes
- The fuels that have a multiplier (x2, x3, x4) instead of a percentage don't affect the Minion's Time Between Actions. Instead, they duplicate each item that the Minion produces.
- In version 0.7, when the [[Catalyst]] was added, the duration was changed from using in-game time to using real time. The actual amount of time didn't change.
- Fuel works even when the player is not online, with the exception of the [[Solar Panel]], which only works with a player on the island.
- The [[Solar Panel]] only works during the day (6AM to 7PM SkyBlock Time), but it can be used in combination with a [[Day Saver]] to have it always work. However, the [[Solar Panel]] will still only work when the player's island is loaded.
- Excluding infinite fuels, [[Enchanted Bread]] is the cheapest fuel by the hour, making it a good choice for new profiles.
- Minion Fuels are additive with other Minion speed upgrades. For example, combining [[Enchanted Coal]] (+10%), a [[Minion Expander]] (+5%), and a [[Floating Crystals|Floating Crystal]] (+10%) results in a total boost of 25%.
- The maximum additive speed boost for a minion is 222%. It can be achieved on a Farming Crop Minion (Wheat, Melon, Pumpkin, Carrot, Potato, Mushroom, Cactus, Cocoa Beans, Sugar Cane, Nether Wart, Sunflower) with the following modifiers:
  - [[Foul Flesh]] (+90%)
  - 2 [[Flycatcher]] (both together +40%)
  - [[Mithril Infusion]] (+10%)
  - [[Free Will]] (+10%)
  - [[Cornucopia Crystal]] (+10%)
    - 16 unique Farming Minion (Wheat, Melon, Pumpkin, Carrot, Potato, Mushroom, Cactus, Cocoa Beans, Sugar Cane, Nether Wart, Sunflower, Cow, Pig, Chicken, Sheep, Rabbit) placed (+16%)
  - [[Postcard]] (+5%)
  - [[Beacon|Beacon V]] (+10%)
    - [[Scorched Power Crystal]] (+1%)
  - Efficient Farming from [[Rabbit Pet]] (+30%)

# Minion Skins
Aliases: Apothejerry Minion Skin, Astronaut Minion Skin, Baby Jerry Minion Skin, Banana Monkey Minion Skin, Basketball Minion Skin, Beach Ball Minion Skin, Bee Minion Skin, Beheaded Minion Skin, Blue Gold Tropical Bird Minion Skin, Bunny Minion Skin, Chef Minion Skin, Choco Rabbit Minion Skin, Clownfish Minion Skin, Cyclops Minion Skin, Easter Egg Minion Skin, Festive Jerry Minion Skin, Festive Skeleton Minion Skin, Festive Zombie Minion Skin, Fish Minion Skin, Fishy Penguin Minion Skin, Fossil Muncher Minion Skin, Ghost Minion Skin, Gingerbread Man Minion Skin, Gingerbread Man Skin, Grinch Minion Skin, Hermit Crab Minion Skin, Hyacinth Tropical Bird Minion Skin, Ice Cream Minion Skin, Ice Lolly Minion Skin, Killer Minion Skin, Lady Bug Minion Skin, Melon Minion Skin, Minion Skin, Mummy Minion Skin, Owl Mage Minion Skin, Owl Minion Skin, Penguin Minion Skin, Pink Bunny Minion Skin, Pink Bunny Personality, Pink Donut Minion Skin, Polar Bear Minion Skin, Present Minion Skin, Pufferfish Minion Skin, Pumpkin Minion Skin, Reindeer Minion Skin, Robot Minion Skin, Sandcastle Minion Skin, Santa Minion Skin, Santa Skin, Scarecrow Minion Skin, Seal Minion Skin, Shark Minion Skin, Skull Minion Skin, Smol Jerry Minion Skin, Snowman Minion Skin, Sun Minion Skin, Tropical Bird 1 Minion Skin, Tropical Bird 2 Minion Skin, Tropical Fish Minion Skin, Undead Minion Skin, White Tiger Minion Skin, Zombie Eye Minion Skin
Categories: Minion Modifiers, Minions, Cosmetics
Minion Skins are [[Rarity|COMMON]] cosmetic items that can be applied to [[Minions]] to change their appearance.
Minion Skins can be placed on a [[Minions|Minion]] by opening their interface and placing it in the Minion Skin slot. Minion Skins can be taken off.

# Minion Upgrades
Aliases: Minion Upgrade, Minion upgrade
Categories: Minion Modifiers
[Minion Upgrades]
Minion Upgrades are items used to affect the minion positively. All of them have a permanent duration and can be taken out at any time.
## Mechanics
Minion Upgrades can be put in the Upgrade Slots of [[Minions]]. A maximum of 2 Minion Upgrade items can be added to a minion.
## List of Minion Upgrades
|Type|Minion Speed|Amount of items|Always on Harvest|Chance|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Replacement Upgrades|✔|✔|✔|100%|Replaces items|
|Spreading Upgrades|✔|✔|✕|Various||
|Independent Cooldown Upgrades|✕|✕|✔|✕|Cooldown between generations|
|Compacting Upgrades|✔|✔|✕|✕|Triggers only when enough items are present in minion after harvest|
|[[Minion Expander]]||||||
|[[Flycatcher]]||||||
|[[Enchanted Egg]]|✔|✕|✔|100%||
|[[Corrupt Soil]]|✔|✔|✔|100%||
|[[Enchanted Shears]]|✔|✕|When spawning|100%||

These upgrades have a chance to generate an item for every item that the minion harvests. The spreading effect triggers from the base drops of the minion. While the island is loaded, the spreading effect also triggers from the [[Egg]] made at spawn of a chicken from a [[Chicken Minion]] with [[Enchanted Egg]]. While the island is unloaded, the spreading effect also triggers from drops of [[Enchanted Egg]], [[Corrupt Soil]], and [[Enchanted Shears]]. While the island is unloaded, the spreading effect triggers only once in an [[Inferno Minion]], even if the used [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] makes more than one drop.
|Icon|Name|Restricted to|Spreading Effect Chance per drop|Spreading Effect Generated item|Obtaining|

|[[Diamond Spreading]]|[[Diamond Spreading]]||0.1 (1 in 10)|[[Diamond]]|Crafting (requires Diamond VI (6) ([[Diamond]])) Recipe: [[Vines]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] → [[Diamond Spreading]]|
|[[Potato Spreading]]|[[Potato Spreading]]|one of any minion|0.05 (1 in 20)|[[Potato]]|1.45% (1 in 69.03 pigs) from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])|
|[[Sleepy Hollow]]|[[Sleepy Hollow]]|one of any minion|0.00015 (1 in 6666.67)|[[Purple Candy]]|Purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] for 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Dark Candy]] during the Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]])|
|[[Krampus Helmet]]|[[Krampus Helmet]]|one of any minion|0.000045 (1 in 22222.22)|[[Red Gift]]|0.10% (1 in 969.12 gifts) from Red Gifts ([[Red Gift]])|
These upgrades generates an item on a cooldown, that is independent of the minion speed. While online, the item will only be generated on a harvest after the cooldown has passed. The cooldown doesn't restart until the item is generated, so while the time between generations isn't affected by Minion Speed, it still relies on it.

|[[Soulflow Engine]]|[[Soulflow Engine]]||90 seconds,; 105 seconds while offline|[[Raw Soulflow]]|Reduce the minion's output by 50%. When placed in a [[Voidling Minion]], the [[Raw Soulflow]] output is increased by 3% per minion tier.|Crafting (requires Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])) Recipe: [[Null Atom]] ×2 + [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x64 + [[Refined Titanium]] x6 ×2 → [[Soulflow Engine]]|

|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|one Farming Minion|3 hours|[[Jacob's Ticket]]||Crafting Recipe: [[Chirping Stereo]] ×2 + [[PlantBoy Advance]] + [[Botroot]] x32 ×2 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x32 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×3 → [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|

Example:; 9 [[Iron Ingot]] → [[Block of Iron]]; 4 [[Glowstone Dust]] → [[Glowstone]]|Crafting (requires Cobblestone V (5) ([[Cobblestone]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] → [[Compactor]]|

This results in two Eggs ([[Egg]]) being made in one cycle while the island is loaded, one on spawn and one on harvest. While the island is unloaded, only one [[Egg]] will be made per cycle.|Crafting (requires Raw Chicken V (5) ([[Raw Chicken]])) Recipe: [[Egg]] x16 ×9 → [[Enchanted Egg]]|
|[[Corrupt Soil]]|[[Corrupt Soil]]|Mob-spawning Minions (Zombie, Revenant, Voidling, Inferno, Vampire, Skeleton, Creeper, Spider, Tarantula, Cave Spider, Blaze, Magma Cube, Enderman, Ghast, Slime, Cow, Pig, Chicken, Sheep, Rabbit)|Make minions spawn [[Corrupted Mobs]], which adds [[Sulphur]] and [[Corrupted Fragment]] to the drops of the mob.; While online, the following minions can make multiple [[Sulphur]] and Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]) per harvest: [[Revenant Minion]], [[Voidling Minion]], [[Skeleton Minion]], [[Tarantula Minion]], [[Magma Cube Minion]], [[Slime Minion]].; [[Inferno Minion]] is not able to make [[Sulphur]] and [[Corrupted Fragment]] while online.|Crafting (requires Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x20 ×8 + [[Corrupted Fragment]] x20 → [[Corrupt Soil]]|
|[[Enchanted Shears]]|[[Enchanted Shears]]|[[Sheep Minion]]|Shears the sheep after they spawn, which gives 1–3 [[Wool]], averaging to 2 [[Wool]] per sheep. Sheared sheep do not drop [[Wool]] when killed.|Crafting Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] ×2 → [[Enchanted Shears]]|
## Tips
- For the spreading upgrades that are limited to one of any minion, [[Snow Minion]] with its high speed and multiple drops per harvest is the best minion option for making the items generated by the spreading upgrades.
  - When AFKing on the island, [[Melon Minion]] is the best option because it can skip spawn actions while the island is loaded.
## Trivia
- [[Potato Spreading]] used to generated an item called "Potatoes" instead of a [[Potato]] due to a bug.
- Despite being able to make drops that can be compacted with a [[Compactor]] in other minions, a [[Compactor]] cannot be placed in [[Revenant Minion|Revenant Minions]], [[Voidling Minion|Voidling Minions]], [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] and [[Tarantula Minion|Tarantula Minions]].
- Putting a [[Diamond Spreading]] in a minion does not make [[Compactor|Compactors]] usable in the minion if it couldn't be used before.

# Lesser Soulflow Engine
Categories: Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Minion Modifiers, Slayer, Enderman Slayer
[Lesser Soulflow Engine]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Obtained: Crafting
Function: Reduces the minion's output by 50%, but it will start generating Soulflow items.
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer II (2) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Soulflow Engine]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 768x (12 stacks (768 items total)) [[Null Sphere]] 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Obsidian]] 15,360x (240 stacks (15,360 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 1,536x (24 stacks (1,536 items total)) [[Blaze Rod]] 204,800x (3,200 stacks (204,800 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LESSER_SOULFLOW_ENGINE
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade.; Placing it in a minion will reduce the minion's output by 50%, but it will start generating Soulflow items.; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 2.; RARE
The Lesser Soulflow Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minions|minion upgrade]] that is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer II (2)]]. It sacrifices [[Minions|minion]] output for [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] item production.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer II (2) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|6x [[Null Ovoid]] 8x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 48x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]]|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] ×6 + [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x24 ×2 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x8 → Lesser Soulflow Engine|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
The Lesser Soulflow Engine requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer II (2)]] to use.
Lesser Soulflow Engine can be placed in all [[Minions]]. When it is added to a minion, the minion's output will be reduced by 50%, but the minion will produce 1 [[Raw Soulflow]] every 180 seconds when player is on the island and every 185 seconds when not.
The [[Raw Soulflow]] production cooldown isn't affected by minion speed modifiers. Drop multiplier [[Minion Fuel|Minion Fuels]] (like [[Hyper Catalyst]]) do increase the amount of [[Raw Soulflow]] made. Purple particles will appear above minions with a Lesser Soulflow Engine.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Soulflow Engine]]|Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|2x [[Null Atom]] [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] 4x Lesser Soulflow Engine 12x [[Refined Titanium]] 64x [[Null Ovoid]]|Recipe: [[Null Atom]] ×2 + Lesser Soulflow Engine ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x64 + [[Refined Titanium]] x6 ×2 → [[Soulflow Engine]]|
## Expected Profits
The following is the expected revenue from 1 minion with a Lesser Soulflow Engine and a [[Super Compactor 3000]], based on the current [[Bazaar]] prices for [[Soulflow (Item)|Soulflow]] and [[Raw Soulflow]].
1 hour: 24 hours
## Trivia
- Minions will wait until the next production cycle to generate [[Raw Soulflow]] if the player is on the island.
- A single minion cannot use 2 Lesser Soulflow Engines at the same time.
- If a minion with a [[Budget Hopper]] or [[Enchanted Hopper]] is full while holding a Lesser Soulflow Engine, the produced [[Raw Soulflow]] will be deleted.
- If the Lesser Soulflow Engine is used for purely getting soulflow, a good choice for minion to use with the upgrade is one that does not fill their storage quickly with their own drops. This leaves more room for soulflow instead. For example:
  - [[Inferno Minion]], which is the slowest minion in the game if not given [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]
  - [[Vampire Minion]], which is the second slowest minion in the game
  - A [[Super Compactor 3000]] with any slow minion whose drops have a second compacted form, e.g. [[Emerald Minion]], [[Quartz Minion]], [[Blaze Minion]].
- If the produced soulflow is used for profit, it is better to use a minion that already makes good profit by itself.
## Gallery
- A Quartz Minion with a Lesser Soulflow Engine (left) beside a Quartz Minion without one (right).

# Acacia Minion

An Acacia Minions is a [[Foraging]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants [[Saplings|Acacia Saplings]] and collects [[Acacia Log|Acacia Logs]]. Acacia Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Acacia Log|Acacia Log I (1)]].

|[[Auto Smelter]]|1x [[Acacia Log]]|[[Coal]]|+0.3 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 1 Coin ([[Coins]]), Stack: NPC: 64 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Acacia Log]]|[[Enchanted Acacia Log]]|+16 [[Foraging]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
|[[Auto Smelter]] with; [[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Coal]]|[[Enchanted Coal]]|+48 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 160 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 10,240 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Coal]]|[[Enchanted Coal Block]]|+7,680 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 25,600 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 1,638,400 [[Coins]]|

## Trivia
- Blaze Minion is one of two minion types that use a bow, along with [[Ghast Minion]].
  - These minions are still crafted with a sword, however.
- A Blaze Minion used to be able to miss their bow shots. Special dialogue existed for when a Blaze Minion missed, such as
  - "They dodged!", or
  - "Woops, I missed it!".

## Bugs
- A Cactus Minion suffers reduced rates if the [[Private Island]] is loaded. This is because the Cactus Minion cannot identify if the cacti are fully grown. Meaning the minion can break 1-block tall cactus alongside 2 & 3-block tall cactus, which makes the harvest not always give 3 [[Cactus]]. The minion generates the proper amount of resources while offline.
- 1 [[Enchanted Cactus Green]] from a Cactus Minion gives 2.5x more [[Farming|Farming XP]] than 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Cactus]], this makes Cactus Minions better than [[Melon Minion|Melon Minions]] for producing [[Farming|Farming XP]].

[Chicken Minion]
Type: Minion

Category: [[Farming]] Minions

A Chicken Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Chicken (Private Island)|Chickens]] and collects [[Raw Chicken]] and [[Feather|Feathers]]. Chicken Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Raw Chicken|Raw Chicken I (1)]].
A Chicken Minion equipped with an [[Enchanted Egg]] also collects [[Egg|Eggs]].

## Bugs
- [[Enchanted Egg]] makes two [[Egg|Eggs]] in one cycle while the island is loaded, one on spawn and one on harvest. While the island is unloaded, only one [[Egg]] will be made per cycle.
- The [[Egg|Eggs]] laid by the [[Chicken|Chickens]] on spawn while the island is loaded are affected by drop multipliers and can trigger upgrade effects (e.g. [[Diamond Spreading]], [[Corrupt Soil]], or [[Raw Soulflow]] production from [[Soulflow Engine]]).
- While the island is loaded, [[Corrupt Soil]] does not make corrupt drops in a Chicken Minion without an [[Enchanted Egg]].

|Feb 28, 2020|Fixed [[Enchanted Egg]] not working while offline.|

## Tips
- While not the best minion for [[Fishing]] XP, a Clay Minion is significantly cheaper than a [[Fishing Minion]] or a [[Lily Pad Minion]].
## Trivia
- The Clay Minion used to be crafted with a pickaxe instead of a shovel.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Tier I-XI Clay Minion.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Reduced time between action by approx 20% each level, down to 16s at level XI.|
|Dec 8, 2022|0.17.1|Changed the [[Wooden Pickaxe]] in the Tier I recipe to a [[Wooden Shovel]].|
|Feb 11, 2025|0.21.3|Added Tier XII.|

A Coal Minion is a [[Mining]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates [[Ores|Coal Ore]] and collects [[Coal]]. Coal Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Coal|Coal I (1)]].
## Usage

A Cobblestone Minion is a [[Mining]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates and collects [[Cobblestone]]. Cobblestone Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Cobblestone|Cobblestone I (1)]].
A Cobblestone Minion is the first type of minion the player encounters. When the player begins SkyBlock, there will be one near [[Jerry]].
## Usage

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
#### Special Layouts
A Cobblestone Minion can be made twice as fast by building a cobblestone generator next to it and covering every other block with something other than cobblestone. This will cause the minion to skip the placing action. However, with too many speed boosts, the minion can be made faster than the generator, which makes the layout not work as well.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

|[[Pets]]||

Aliases: Cocoa Beans Minion I, Cocoa Beans Minion II, Cocoa Beans Minion III, Cocoa Beans Minion IV, Cocoa Beans Minion IX, Cocoa Beans Minion V, Cocoa Beans Minion VI, Cocoa Beans Minion VII, Cocoa Beans Minion VIII, Cocoa Beans Minion X, Cocoa Beans Minion XI, Cocoa Beans Minion XII, Cocoa Minion

Category: [[Farming]] Minions

[Metadata]
ID: COCOA_GENERATOR_1; COCOA_GENERATOR_2; COCOA_GENERATOR_3; COCOA_GENERATOR_4; COCOA_GENERATOR_5; COCOA_GENERATOR_6; COCOA_GENERATOR_7; COCOA_GENERATOR_8; COCOA_GENERATOR_9; COCOA_GENERATOR_10; COCOA_GENERATOR_11; COCOA_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting cocoa beans. Requires jungle logs nearby so cocoa can be placed, or air nearby so jungle logs can be placed. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 27s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Cocoa Beans Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Cocoa Beans]]. Cocoa Beans Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Cocoa Beans|Cocoa Beans I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 3; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Cocoa Beans]]|+0.2 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 3 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 192 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Cocoa Beans]]|[[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]]|+32 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 480 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 30,720 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]]|[[Enchanted Cookie]]|+5,120 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 76,800 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 4,915,200 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
The [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] in the ideal layout are stacked to 3 blocks tall. A Cocoa Beans Minion can place missing [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] (see #Trivia).

## Trivia
- A Cocoa Beans Minion can place missing [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] for the ideal layout, these logs give [[Foraging|Foraging XP]] and [[Jungle Log]] [[Collections|Collection]] when cut down.
  - A Cocoa Beans Minion prioritizes planting cocoa beans in available spots over placing missing logs.
  - The ideal layout for a Cocoa Beans Minion with [[Minion Expander|Minion Expanders]] is the same 8 pillars but in the corners and sides of the expanded area.
  - A Cocoa Beans Minions recognizes the spawning spots around [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] placed by the player, even when not placed according to the ideal layout.
  - Cocoa Beans Minions are fine with cocoa beans obstructing the locations for [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] in the ideal layout.
  - A Cocoa Beans Minion wants specifically [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] and not any other type of wood.
- Cocoa Beans Minion is the only minion type that can place blocks that are also generated by a different minion type ( [[Jungle Minion]]).

|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Now works with [[Farm Crystal]].|
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Now has a 12th Tier.|

## Trivia
- Before the 0.19.10 update, this minion was the only way to get Creeper bestiary, as Creepers could not spawn naturally on the [[Private Island]]. This was changed after the 0.19.9 update prevented gaining bestiary from mobs spawned by minions, which had made it impossible to get Creeper bestiary.

### Minion Modifiers
Some [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] can only be applied to a limited selection of [[Minions]]. The following of which can be applied to an Enderman Minion.
|Modifier Type|Affects Enderman Minion|
|-|-|
|Fuel ([[Minion Fuel]])||
|Upgrades ([[Minion Upgrades]])|[[Corrupt Soil]]|
|Crystals ([[Minion Crystal]])||
|[[Pets]]||
|Mayors ([[Mayor Election]])||
The following modifiers can be applied to all minions.
|Modifier Type|Affects all [[Minions]]|
|-|-|
|Fuel ([[Minion Fuel]])|[[Block of Coal]], [[Catalyst]], [[Charcoal]], [[Coal]], [[Enchanted Bread]], [[Enchanted Charcoal]], [[Enchanted Coal]], [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]], [[Everburning Flame]], [[Foul Flesh]], [[Hamster Wheel]], [[Hyper Catalyst]], [[Magma Bucket]], [[Plasma Bucket]], [[Solar Panel]], [[Tasty Cheese]]|

|Upgrades ([[Minion Upgrades]])|[[Diamond Spreading]], [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], [[Flycatcher]], [[Krampus Helmet]], [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]], [[Minion Expander]], [[Potato Spreading]], [[Sleepy Hollow]], [[Soulflow Engine]], [[Super Compactor 3000]]|
|Mayors ([[Mayor Election]])|[[Derpy]]|
|Misc.|[[Beacon]], [[Free Will]], [[Minion Storage]], [[Mithril Infusion]], [[Postcard]]|
### Stranded
On the [[Stranded]] gamemode, [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] can be used as an upgrade, allowing the minion to spawn and kill [[Zealot|Zealots]] instead of enderman but makes the minion twice as slow. However instead of collecting 1 enderpearl, the minion collects 5-8 enderpearls, 1-2 endstone, and loot from chest Zealots although this is more rare.

# Fishing Minion
Aliases: Fish Minion, Fish minion, Fishing Minion 1, Fishing Minion I, Fishing Minion II, Fishing Minion III, Fishing Minion IV, Fishing Minion IX, Fishing Minion V, Fishing Minion VI, Fishing Minion VII, Fishing Minion VIII, Fishing Minion X, Fishing Minion XI, Fishing Minion XII

Category: [[Fishing]] Minions

ID: FISHING_GENERATOR_1; FISHING_GENERATOR_2; FISHING_GENERATOR_3; FISHING_GENERATOR_4; FISHING_GENERATOR_5; FISHING_GENERATOR_6; FISHING_GENERATOR_7; FISHING_GENERATOR_8; FISHING_GENERATOR_9; FISHING_GENERATOR_10; FISHING_GENERATOR_11; FISHING_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start fishing. Requires water nearby.; Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 75s Max Storage: 640; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Fishing Minion is a [[Fishing]] [[Minions|Minion]] that collects fish and other fishing drops. Fishing Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod II (2)]].
A Fishing Minion can only perform harvest actions.

## Trivia
- A Fishing Minion is the fourth slowest [[Minions|minion]] in the game when at tier 1, taking 75 seconds to perform an action.
- A Fishing Minion has the second largest amount of unique drops at 7 different items. A [[Flower Minion]] has more with 13 different items.
- Upgrading a Fishing Minion from Tier I to Tier II provides no buffs at all.
- Tier VI and VII used to have the same texture.
- Because a Fishing Minion only harvests, the formula for harvests per minute becomes 60seconds between actions.

# Flower Minion
Aliases: Flower Minion I, Flower Minion II, Flower Minion III, Flower Minion IV, Flower Minion IX, Flower Minion V, Flower Minion VI, Flower Minion VII, Flower Minion VIII, Flower Minion X, Flower Minion XI, Flower Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Craftable items, Dark Auction
[Flower Minion]
Type: Minion
Category: [[Foraging]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x Dandelion ([[Flowers]])(35%) 1x Poppy ([[Flowers]])(15%) 1x Allium ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Azure Bluet ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Blue Orchid ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Peony ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Oxeye Daisy ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Rose Bush ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Lilac ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Red Tulip ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x White Tulip ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Pink Tulip ([[Flowers]])(4.545%) 1x Orange Tulip ([[Flowers]])(4.545%)
Upgrade with: Dandelion ([[Flowers]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] [[Enchanted Poppy]]
[Metadata]
ID: FLOWER_GENERATOR_1; FLOWER_GENERATOR_2; FLOWER_GENERATOR_3; FLOWER_GENERATOR_4; FLOWER_GENERATOR_5; FLOWER_GENERATOR_6; FLOWER_GENERATOR_7; FLOWER_GENERATOR_8; FLOWER_GENERATOR_9; FLOWER_GENERATOR_10; FLOWER_GENERATOR_11; FLOWER_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting flowers.; Requires grass nearby so flowers can be planted. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 30s Max Storage: 960; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Flower Minion is a [[Foraging]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects various [[Flowers]]. A Flower Minion I can be bought from the [[Dark Auction]] for a minimum of 2,500,000 [[Coins]]. Flower Minion recipes, except Flower Minion XII, are unlocked by default. Flower Minion XII recipe is unlocked after reaching [[Museum/Milestones|Museum Milestone 10]].
[[Flowers]] planted by a Flower Minion are a [[Flowers|Dandelion]], a [[Flowers|Poppy]], or a randomly selected flower (excluding [[Flowers|Dandelions]], [[Flowers|Poppies]], and [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]]).
A Flower Minion equipped with [[Thorny Vines]] plants and collects [[Wild Rose|Wild Roses]].

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
#### Special Layouts
A Flower Minion can be made to not spawn 2 block tall flowers by placing a roof of any non-air block above the minion's spawning area. This does not affect the rate of [[Flowers|Dandelions]] and [[Flowers|Poppies]].
A Flower Minion can be made almost twice as fast by letting water break the flowers and push them into [[Hopper|Hoppers]]. To let the Flower Minion have time to plant flowers, the water needs to be on a timer which can be created with a [[Cobblestone Minion]] and a cobblestone generator. Through a series of water pipes, the water from the timer can be spread to all Flower Minions. This will cause the minion to skip the harvesting action, but that does make the minion 10% slower[Confirm (Confirmation needed; is every planting action made slower?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]. On top of that, the water can linger for too long, blocking the minion, so it is almost twice as fast.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Tutorial on building this layout; When this info is added, remove this.)
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

### Quests
A Flower Minion is required on step 9 of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest. Any tier works and it will not be returned.

## Tips
- All flowers can be sold to NPC for 1 [[Coins|Coin]]. However flowers that are 2 blocks high can be turned into 2 of their specific dyes, which sells for 2 [[Coins]] total.
## Trivia
- Flower Minion recipes can be viewed in-game using /viewrecipe FLOWER_GENERATOR_2, even if the player doesn't have the minion unlocked.
- A Flower Minion has the largest amount of possible drops at 13 different items.
- Flower Minion is the only [[Minions|Minion]] type to have every storage slot unlocked upon obtaining.
- Flower Minion is the only [[Minions|Minion]] type to not be upgraded to tier XII via a purchase in an [[NPC]] shop.
- A Flower Minion is the only item in a [[Dark Auction]] guaranteed to appear.
- When surrounded by [[Dirt]], [[Podzol]] and [[Coarse Dirt]] a Flower Minion does not complain. It will also not produce flowers.

# Ghast Minion
Aliases: Ghast Minion I, Ghast Minion II, Ghast Minion III, Ghast Minion IV, Ghast Minion IX, Ghast Minion V, Ghast Minion VI, Ghast Minion VII, Ghast Minion VIII, Ghast Minion X, Ghast Minion XI, Ghast Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Ghast Tear Collection
[Ghast Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Ghast Tear I (1) ([[Ghast Tear]])
Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x [[Ghast Tear]]
Upgrade with: [[Ghast Tear]] [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] [[Silver Fang]]
[Metadata]
ID: GHAST_GENERATOR_1; GHAST_GENERATOR_2; GHAST_GENERATOR_3; GHAST_GENERATOR_4; GHAST_GENERATOR_5; GHAST_GENERATOR_6; GHAST_GENERATOR_7; GHAST_GENERATOR_8; GHAST_GENERATOR_9; GHAST_GENERATOR_10; GHAST_GENERATOR_11; GHAST_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and slaying Ghasts!; BEWARE OF EXPLOSIONS! Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 50s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Ghast Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Ghast (Minion Mob)|Ghasts]] and collects [[Ghast Tear|Ghast Tears]]. Ghast Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Ghast Tear|Ghast Tear I (1)]].

## Tips
- It is highly recommended that one builds a 10x10x10 box for this minion. If there is no box around the minion, the Ghasts may fly away and roam around the island.
## Trivia
- Ghast Minion is one of two minion types who use a bow, along with [[Blaze Minion]].
  - These minions are still crafted with a sword, however.
- A Ghast Minion used to be able to miss its bow shots. Special dialogue existed for when a Ghast Minion missed, such as
  - "Wait for it", or
  - "I should have chosen a different job".
- Timedeo and his friends abused the method of using Ghast Minions to gain lots and lots of [[Combat]] XP. Eventually, the admins disabled getting combat xp on private islands.

# Gravel Minion
Aliases: Gravel Minion I, Gravel Minion II, Gravel Minion III, Gravel Minion IV, Gravel Minion IX, Gravel Minion V, Gravel Minion VI, Gravel Minion VII, Gravel Minion VIII, Gravel Minion X, Gravel Minion XI, Gravel Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Confirmations needed, Craftable items, Gravel Collection, Mining
[Gravel Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Gravel I (1) ([[Gravel]])
Category: [[Mining]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x [[Gravel]]
Upgrade with: [[Gravel]] [[Enchanted Flint]]
[Metadata]
ID: GRAVEL_GENERATOR_1; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_2; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_3; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_4; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_5; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_6; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_7; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_8; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_9; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_10; GRAVEL_GENERATOR_11
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and mining gravel!; Requires an open area to place gravel. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 26s Max Storage: 128; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Gravel Minion is a [[Mining]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates and collects [[Gravel]]. Gravel Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Gravel|Gravel I (1)]].
A Gravel Minion equipped with a [[Flint Shovel]] collects [[Flint]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Gravel]]|+0.2 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 3 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 192 [[Coins]]|
|[[Flint Shovel]]|1x [[Gravel]]|[[Flint]]|+0.2 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 4 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 256 [[Coins]]|
|[[Flint Shovel]] with; [[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Flint]]|[[Enchanted Flint]]|+32 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 640 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 40,960 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.
While the island is loaded, a Gravel Minion without a [[Flint Shovel]] has a 10% chance to collect [[Flint]] instead of [[Gravel]]. While the island is not loaded, it never collects flint without a [[Flint Shovel]].
### Stats
|Tier|Base Time; between Actions|Storage|Per day Items|Per day NPC Profit|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|26 seconds|128|1,662x (25 stacks plus 62 (1,662 in total)) [[Flint]]|6,646.15 [[Coins]]|
|II|26 seconds|256|1,662x [[Flint]]|6,646.15 [[Coins]]|
|III|24 seconds|256|1,800x (28 stacks plus 8 (1,800 in total)) [[Flint]]|7,200 [[Coins]]|
|IV|24 seconds|384|1,800x [[Flint]]|7,200 [[Coins]]|
|V|22 seconds|384|1,964x (30 stacks plus 44 (1,964 in total)) [[Flint]]|7,854.55 [[Coins]]|
|VI|22 seconds|576|1,964x [[Flint]]|7,854.55 [[Coins]]|
|VII|19 seconds|576|2,274x (35 stacks plus 34 (2,274 in total)) [[Flint]]|9,094.74 [[Coins]]|
|VIII|19 seconds|768|2,274x [[Flint]]|9,094.74 [[Coins]]|
|IX|16 seconds|768|2,700x (42 stacks plus 12 (2,700 in total)) [[Flint]]|10,800 [[Coins]]|
|X|16 seconds|960|2,700x [[Flint]]|10,800 [[Coins]]|
|XI|13 seconds|960|3,323x (51 stacks plus 59 (3,323 in total)) [[Flint]]|13,292.31 [[Coins]]|
Note: A [[Flint Shovel]] is used in the above profit calculation. Values shown are for when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.
### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
Due to [[Gravel]] being affected by gravity, there needs to be a block under the air to keep the gravel in reach.
#### Special Layouts
A Gravel Minion can be made almost twice as fast by letting the gravel fall on a non-full block (like [[Sign|Signs]] or [[Carpet]]) above a [[Hopper]]. The non-full block breaks the falling gravel and hoppers underneath can collect it. This will cause the minion to skip the mining action, but that does make the minion 10% slower[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], so it is almost twice as fast.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

|[[Pets]]||

## Bugs
- [[Hard Stone]] produced by Hard Stone Minions does not give any skill xp.
  - [[Enchanted Hard Stone]] still gives skill xp.
- [[Stone]] placed by Hard Stone Minions are no longer custom blocks.
  - When mined by a player, the blocks will drop [[Cobblestone]] (or [[Stone]] with [[Silk Touch]]) rather than nothing with the message (see Trivia).
## Trivia
- Hard Stone Minions will place [[Stone]] around them to serve as [[Hard Stone]].
  - Mining the blocks manually will not drop [[Hard Stone]] and instead results in the following message: Drops from custom blocks are currently disabled on private islands!. This is likely because of a glitch that allowed minion-spawned [[Hard Stone]] to be mined much faster than intended.
  - This is currently bugged (see Bugs).

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
If the block light (light produced by blocks instead of the sky) level next to the ice is higher than 11, the ice can melt into water. An Ice Minion will stop working if its spawning area is filled with water. This can be avoided by using an [[Ice Cube]] which prevents ice from melting.
#### Special Layouts
An Ice Minion can be made almost twice as fast by setting the biome to a cold biome and adding water sources[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; exact places of water sources; When this info is added, remove this.) that spread to where the minion broke ice. The water that floods the spawning area will freeze to ice. This will cause the minion to skip the generating action. However, because the freezing is random, it is not consistently twice as fast. Make sure of the following when building this special layout:
- The spawning area has a dry area for the minion to work normally when no water has frozen yet.
- Some water sources have blocks above them to stop them from freezing. Otherwise if all the water freezes, the special layout stop working.
- The water that needs to freeze is exposed to the sky. Otherwise it will not freeze.
- Ice melting from [[Ice Cube]] is turned on (/islandproperties), as turning it off stops random ticks which is needed for water to freeze.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

# Inferno Minion
Aliases: Inferno Minion I, Inferno Minion II, Inferno Minion III, Inferno Minion IV, Inferno Minion IX, Inferno Minion V, Inferno Minion VI, Inferno Minion VII, Inferno Minion VIII, Inferno Minion X, Inferno Minion XI, Inferno Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Slayer, Blaze Slayer, Slayer Minions
[Inferno Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])
Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x [[Crude Gabagool]]
Upgrade with: [[Derelict Ashe]] [[Molten Powder]] [[Inferno Vertex]] [[Inferno Apex]]
[Metadata]
ID: INFERNO_GENERATOR_1; INFERNO_GENERATOR_2; INFERNO_GENERATOR_3; INFERNO_GENERATOR_4; INFERNO_GENERATOR_5; INFERNO_GENERATOR_6; INFERNO_GENERATOR_7; INFERNO_GENERATOR_8; INFERNO_GENERATOR_9; INFERNO_GENERATOR_10; INFERNO_GENERATOR_11
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and slaying Infernals!; Minions also work when you are offline!; Ability: Rising Celsius Each Inferno minion increases the speed of ALL Inferno minions by 18%, up to 180%.; Time Between Action: 1013s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
An Inferno Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Infernal (Mob)|Infernals (Mob)]] and collects [[Crude Gabagool]]. Inferno Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL III (3)]].
The minion is tradeable and may be used without [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL III (3)]], but it cannot be crafted or upgraded without meeting the slayer requirement.
An Inferno Minion is normally extremely slow. However its speed can be boosted by its ability Rising Celsius (up to 180%) and [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] (10x, 15x, or 20x) to around the average of other minions. [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] also lets the minion have other drops.
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Crude Gabagool]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 1 Coin ([[Coins]]), Stack: NPC: 64 [[Coins]]|
|Any LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 0.007352; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 136|[[Chili Pepper]]|Per Item: NPC: 5,000 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 320,000 [[Coins]]|
|Any LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 0.0001681; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 5950|[[Inferno Vertex]]|Per Item, Stack|
|Any LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 7.639e-07; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 1309091|[[Inferno Apex]]|Per Item, Stack|
|Any LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 2.183e-06; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 458182|[[Reaper Pepper]]|Per Item, Stack|
|Any LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]); [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 2.546e-07; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 3927273|[[Gabagool the Fish]]|Per Item, Stack|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|192x [[Crude Gabagool]]|[[Very Crude Gabagool]]|Per Item: NPC: 1 Coin ([[Coins]]), Stack: NPC: 64 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.
#### Inferno Minion Fuel
The [[Crude Gabagool]] base drop of an Inferno Minion can be replaced by using [[Inferno Minion Fuel]]. The [[Distillates|Distillate]] used in crafting the [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] determines what item the Inferno Minion will specialise in creating, either [[Magma Cream]], [[Blaze Rod]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Glowstone Dust]], or [[Crude Gabagool]]. The distillate's output will replace the normal output 80% of time the minion makes a harvest.
Giving fuel created with [[Hypergolic Gabagool]] to an Inferno Minion lets it very rarely produce special items ( [[Chili Pepper]], [[Inferno Vertex]], [[Inferno Apex]], [[Reaper Pepper]], [[Gabagool the Fish]]). Applying [[Capsaicin Eyedrops]] to the minion will increase the chance of these rare items by 30% for 24 hours. An Inferno Minion X and an Inferno Minion XI will create an additional [[Inferno Apex]] upon creating one, because of their Apex Minion ability.

## Tips
- As a budget setup, a player can use 9 tier I-II Inferno Minions to boost the one of highest tier.
## Bugs
- The speed multipliers from [[Inferno Minion Fuel|Inferno Minion Fuels]] are applied along with the other speed buffs to the base action time as follows:baseActionTime(1+speedBuffs)*(1+InfernoMinionFuelMultiplier). This extra 1+ in front of the Inferno Minion Fuels multiplier causes the effective multipliers to be 11x, 16x, and 21x.
## Trivia
- Inferno Minion is the slowest minion type in Normal mode.
- Inferno Minion is the only minion type:
  - That has an ability.
  - That requires itself to be crafted twice, rather than once; or itself and another minion.
  - That cannot be quickcrafted into the next tier when placed.
- In versions before SkyBlock 0.19.5, the Apex Minion ability was bugged and only created the two [[Inferno Apex]] if the [[Private Island]] is loaded (for example if the player is AFK). If not, then it would've generated only one. It was theorized that while offline, the minion attempted to stack the two together, which is considered invalid, thus the second is deleted to become a single item.
- Inferno Minion has the lowest slayer level requirement of all slayer specific minion types.
- Without using [[Inferno Minion Fuel|Inferno Minion Fuels]] created with [[Hypergolic Gabagool]], players can only get to tier VIII of Inferno Minion without buying [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]] from other players.
- The production rate of [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]] was designed to be "a couple Inferno Vertices a day" with a "maxed" setup.
- It used to be a common belief that the rare drops of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] rolled once per item, rather than once per harvest, this has been proven false. This resulted in the [[Distillates|Nether Wart Distillate]] being more popular at the time.
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Inferno Minion Fuel|Inferno Minion Fuels]] can be stacked inside an Inferno Minion despite not normally being stackable, and will have the fuel time match the amount stacked.
- Before the minion backend update on April 9, 2025, only one [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]] would appear after all the fuel was depleted, even if multiple were inserted.
- Contrary to a common belief, while the [[Private Island]] is not loaded, all drops (including the rare drops) from an Inferno Minion are affected by [[Derpy]]'s TURBO MINIONS!!! ability.
  - This common belief likely originated from a time altering bug of TURBO MINIONS!!! (see [[Derpy|Derpy#Bugs]]).

# Lily Pad Minion
Aliases: Lily Pad Minion I, Lily Pad Minion II, Lily Pad Minion III, Lily Pad Minion IV, Lily Pad Minion IX, Lily Pad Minion V, Lily Pad Minion VI, Lily Pad Minion VII, Lily Pad Minion VIII, Lily Pad Minion X, Lily Pad Minion XI, Lily Pad Minion XII, Lilypad Minion, Lilypad minion
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Lily Pad Collection
[Lily Pad Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Lily Pad I (1) ([[Lily Pad]])
Category: [[Fishing]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x [[Lily Pad]]
Upgrade with: [[Lily Pad]] [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] [[Condensed Lily Pad]] [[Lily Pad Minion XII Upgrade Stone]]
[Metadata]
ID: LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_1; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_2; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_3; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_4; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_5; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_6; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_7; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_8; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_9; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_10; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_11; LILY_PAD_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting lily pads.; Requires water nearby. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 37.5s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Lily Pad Minion is a [[Fishing]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates and collects [[Lily Pad|Lily Pads]]. Lily Pad Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Lily Pad|Lily Pad I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Lily Pad]]|+0.8 [[Fishing]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 10 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 640 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Lily Pad]]|[[Enchanted Lily Pad]]|+128 [[Fishing]]|Per Item: NPC: 1,600 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 102,400 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Lily Pad]]|[[Condensed Lily Pad]]|+20,480 [[Fishing]]|Per Item: NPC: 256,000 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 16,384,000 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.
